[飞书用户 用户]: 📝 论文自检任务：缩写/专有名词定义检查

请对当前论文做一次缩写定义自检。AI 写作常见问题：很多专有名词的缩写（acronym）从第一次出现就没给出全称，整篇论文都默认读者认识。这不符合学术写作规范。

自检要求：

1. 通读全文，列出所有缩写词（包括方法名、数据集名、指标名、模型名）
2. 对每个缩写，确认它在【第一次出现时】用了 "全称 (缩写)" 格式
   ✅ 正确：Large Language Model (LLM) ... 后续可直接用 LLM
   ❌ 错误：摘要里用 LLM，但全文从未给出 Large Language Model
3. 特别关注以下高发区：
   - 你自己造的方法名/算法名（例如 SCALE、TRACE、PIVOT 这类）——是首字母缩写还是单纯命名？如果是缩写，必须说明展开
   - 数据集/benchmark 名（MMLU、ARC、GSM8K 等常见 benchmark 也要在首次出现时给全称）
   - 指标缩写（F1、AUC、BLEU、ROUGE-L 等）
   - 领域术语（RLHF、DPO、CoT、ICL、PEFT 等）
4. Abstract 和 Introduction 是首次出现的高发区，Abstract 里的缩写也要在 Abstract 里展开一次

修完后请简短汇报修了哪些。

Page 8 is particularly loose with the heatmap and stacked bar followed by a sparse Conclusion section.

The strongest moves would be: relocating the D-study figure to page 4 where that analysis is actually discussed, adding a variance decomposition chart on page 6 to highlight that item_id drives 58.4% of the variance, shifting the difficulty scatter plot earlier to page 6, and moving the stacked bar chart up as well.

Actually, I'm reconsidering the layout strategy. The real issue is that methods sections typically don't have data figures, but I can work around this by creating figures that illustrate methodological structure rather than results. I should add a schematic on page 4 showing the factorial design with all the crossed factors, move the budget sensitivity analysis from the appendix to page 5 since it validates the G-coefficient estimates, and then create a variance breakdown bar chart for page 6 to visualize the single-model baseline findings.

Now I'm ready to draft the detailed instructions for the Agent to execute this reorganization.
